The `aeronDirectoryName` of the contexts is not set (no Aeron directory, D4). |
| `test/system/MultiDriverTest.test.ts` | all | As `MaxFlowControlStrategySystemTest`; `CountDownLatch newImageLatch` is a flag set by the available image handler and `newImageLatch.await()` is `await tests.drainUntil(() => newImage)`; `while (publication.offer(..) < 0L) Tests.yield()` is `drainUntil(() => offer >= 0)`. |
| `test/system/PubAndSubTest.test.ts` | all | As the other system tests (D115, spec D3): `TestMediaDriver.launch` is `SystemTests.launch` with a manual clock (1 ms per turn), the clients are `TestClient`s of the node (`clientContext.clone()` twice is the first client of `launch` and `tests.connect`), `Tests.yield()` loops are `drainUntil` and `Tests.executeUntil(.., MAX_VALUE, 500 ms)` is `tests.executeUntil`; `mock(FragmentHandler.class)` is a `FragmentRecorder` (`test/support/FragmentRecorder.ts`) whose recorded lengths replace `verify(.., times(n)).onFragment(any, anyInt, eq(length), any)` and the `InOrder` of `shouldContinueAfterRolloverWithMinimalPaddingHeader` (the whole sequence is compared, which is stricter); the image handlers are `vi.fn` objects; `RethrowingErrorHandler.INSTANCE` is a handler that throws; `watcher` checks the error log, which is `unexpectedErrors()` (the driver and client errors, minus those `ignoreErrorsMatching` names). The `aeron:ipc` row of each `channels()` test skips at run time. The tests that Java launches with `aeron:ipc` only to get a driver and two clients (`shouldAllowSubscriptionsIfUsingTagsAndParametersAndAllMatch` and the nine `...Reject...`/`...Allow...` tag tests) launch with no channel: the subscription and publication of the launch channel are not used by them. `shouldReceivePublishedMessageViaPollFile`: `rawPoll` is asserted to deliver one block at offset 0 of the expected length; `assertTrue(channel.isOpen())` has no file channel to check (spec D4, `RawBlockHandler.fileChannel` is `unknown`). `shouldReceivePublishedMessageOneForOneWithDataLoss` and `...BatchedWithDataLoss`: `TestMediaDriver.enableRandomLoss` is in `test/support/SystemTests.ts` (the debug receive endpoint with `RandomLossGenerator`, seed `0xcafebabe`), and `verifyLossOccurredForStream(aeronDirectoryName, streamId)` reads the entries of an in-memory `LossReport` that the test sets with `context.lossReport(..)` (spec D50: no loss report file). `shouldCleanupImagesWhenPublicationIsClosed` and `verifyLogBufferType`: `Files.exists(.../publications/<id>.logbuffer)` is `MemoryLogFactory.lookup(name) !== null`, and `new LogBuffers(path)` is `new LogBuffers(name, logFactory)` (spec D35). `shouldMarkPublicationNotConnectedWhenItLoosesAllSubscribers` measures with the manual clock (`System.nanoTime()` is `clock.nanoTime()`). `ThreadLocalRandom` is `mulberry32` with a fixed seed. `@EnumSource(ThreadingMode.class)` runs the four modes, which all launch as `INVOKER` (D3); `driverAgentInvoker` and `invokeInvokers()` are `tests.cycle()` inside `drainUntil`. Counter ids and types are those of `AeronCounters`; `findByTypeIdAndRegistrationId` and `getCounterOwnerId` are `CountersReader`'s. |
| `test/system/MinFlowControlSystemTest.test.ts` | all | As the other system tests (D115): `TestMediaDriver.launch` is `SystemTests.launch` with a manual clock (1 ms per turn), so the 1000 ms receiver timeout, the 500 ms image liveness and the 100 ms timer are turns; `@TempDir` and `aeronDirectoryName` have no meaning (no Aeron directory, D4); the driver C of `shouldPreventConnectionUntilGroupMinSizeIsMet` is a third `TestNode`; `Tests.sleep(1)` and `Tests.yield()` loops are `drainUntil` or a turn (`tests.cycle()` and an event loop turn); `mock(FragmentHandler.class)` is a `FragmentRecorder` and `verify(h, times(n)).onFragment(.., eq(MESSAGE_LENGTH), ..)` its `count(MESSAGE_LENGTH)`; `fail(..)` is a thrown `Error`; every loop has a cap in turns; the `@MethodSource` rows are `[supplier or null, uri parameters]`; `FlowControlTests` is `test/support/FlowControlTests.ts`; `flowControlGroupMinSize` is the context's; the `try`/`finally` that closes the resources is the teardown of the test. |
| `test/system/TaggedFlowControlSystemTest.test.ts` | all | As `MinFlowControlSystemTest`; the group tags are `number`s in the driver contexts and `bigint`s in `ChannelUriStringBuilder.groupTag` / `taggedFlowControl` (`2701L` is `2701n`); `Tests.yieldingIdle(..)` is a turn; the `State` class and `pollWithTimeout` are kept, and each loop has a cap that fails with the state, where Java's `yieldingIdle` fails when the test is interrupted. |
| `test/system/ChannelEndpointStatusTest.test.ts` | all | `ThreadingMode.DEDICATED` is `INVOKER` (D3); the driver error handler ignores `Address already in use` as Java's and its Node text, `EADDRINUSE`; the mocked client error handlers are not used by any test and are not made; the third client `clientC` is `tests.connect(driverB)`; `testWatcher.ignoreErrorsMatching(s -> true)` means the errors are not asserted; `TestMediaDriver.notSupportedOnCMediaDriver` takes the Java branch; `fail("Should have thrown an exception")` is the failed `toBeInstanceOf(RegistrationException)` of the caught value. |
| `test/system/MultiDestinationCastTest.test.ts` | all | As `MinFlowControlSystemTest`; `CountDownLatch` is a flag set by the image handler and `await()` is `drainUntil`; `System.currentTimeMillis()` windows (`500`, `1_000`, `2_000` ms of observation) are that many turns; `pollForFragment` is `tests.executeUntil` (which stops at its limits and does not fail, as Java's); `CountingFragmentHandler` is `test/support/CountingFragmentHandler.ts`; `addDestinationWithSpySubscriptionsShouldFailWithRegistrationException` runs (the refusal is the driver's: `Aeron spies are invalid as send destinations`); `mock(ErrorHandler.class)` is `{ onError: vi.fn() }`; `BitUtil.generateRandomisedId()` is the port's. |
| `test/system/MultiDestinationSubscriptionTest.test.ts` | all | As `MultiDestinationCastTest`; `launchSecond()` is a second `TestNode`; closing and reconnecting `clientA` in `subscriptionCloseShouldAlsoCloseMediaDriverPorts` is `TestClient.close()` and `tests.connect(driverA)`; `@EnabledOnOs(OS.LINUX)` is a `ctx.skip` on other platforms; `Long.numberOfTrailingZeros(termBufferLength)` is `LogBufferDescriptor.positionBitsToShift`; `shouldNotReuseEndpointAcrossMultipleSubscriptionsIfAtLeastOneIsMds` closes the subscriptions of each `try` block in reverse order at its end, and its `errorMatcher` accepts Node's `EADDRINUSE` for the JVM's `Address already in use`; `ThreadLocalRandom` is `mulberry32`; the counters scan of `CountersReader.forEach(MetaData)` is a loop over the counter ids. |
| `test/system/MdsAndMdcInteractionTest.test.ts` | all | As `MultiDestinationSubscriptionTest`; `Aeron.connect` of the leader and the follower are `tests.connect(driver)` (the driver's first client is not used); the end of a `try (Aeron follower = ...; Subscription lateJoinSub = ...)` block is `lateJoinSub.close()` and `follower.close()`; `catchupPub` is closed with `close()`; `offer(msg)` is the whole buffer. |

## Renames

| Java | TypeScript | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| `BitUtil.findNextPositivePowerOfTwo(long)`, `align(long, long)`, `isEven(long)`, `isPowerOfTwo(long)` | `findNextPositivePowerOfTwoLong`, `alignLong`, `isEvenLong`, `isPowerOfTwoLong` | `int` and `long` are both `number`; overloads that cannot be told apart get the Java name with a `Long` suffix (spec 5.1). |
| `AsciiEncoding.digitCount(long)`, `endOffset(long)` | `digitCountLong`, `endOffsetLong` | Same rule. They accept `number \| bigint`: a `bigint` reaches the 19-digit values (addition). |
| `BitUtil.toHexByteArray` (3 overloads), `toHex` (2 overloads) | `toHexByteArray(buffer: Uint8Array \| string, offset?, length?)`, `toHex(buffer, offset?, length?)` | Overloads that differ in arity or in `byte[]` / `CharSequence` become one method with optional parameters and a union. |
| `MediaDriver.Context` | `MediaDriverContext` (exported from `src/driver/MediaDriver.ts`), also `MediaDriver.Context` for the value and the type | A nested class is a top-level export with a static member on the outer class, as `UdpChannel.Context` is `UdpChannelContext` and `CommonContext.InferableBoolean` is `InferableBoolean`. |
| the fields of `MediaDriver.Context` (`mtuLength`, `errorHandler`, ...) | `_mtuLength`, `_errorHandler`, ... | A class cannot have a field and a method of one name, and the accessors keep the Java names (as `UdpChannelContext`, with a trailing `_`; here the leading `_` is the convention of the other ported classes). |
| a getter and its fluent setter (`mtuLength()` and `mtuLength(int)`) in `MediaDriver.Context` and `CommonContext` | one method `mtuLength(): number` and `mtuLength(value: number): this` | Overloads that differ in arity are one method (spec 5.1). The setters return `this`, which stands for the covariant overrides `Context aeronDirectoryName(...)` and the others in Java. |
| `Integer.getInteger`, `Long.getLong`, `Boolean.getBoolean`, `System.getProperty` / `setProperty` / `clearProperty`, `Math.multiplyExact(long, long)` | `Integer.getInteger`, `Long.getLong`, `Boolean.getBoolean` (`jdk.ts`), `System` (`jdkLang.ts`), `multiplyExact` (`jdk.ts`) | The JDK stand-ins (spec D22, D23). |
| `AsciiEncoding.isDigit(byte)` / `isDigit(char)`, `getDigit(int, byte)` / `getDigit(int, char)` | `isDigit(value)`, `getDigit(index, value)` | A `byte` and a `char` are both `number`. |
| `LangUtil.rethrowUnchecked(Throwable)` returns `void` | returns `never` | Lets the compiler see that control does not continue. |
| `ErrorHandler.onError(Throwable)` | `onError(throwable: Error)` | `Throwable` is `Error`. |
| (none) | `longToNumber`, `longValueExact`, `Long.and`, `Long.numberOfLeadingZeros(number \| bigint)`, `TimeUnit`, `Integer.parseInt`, `Long.parseLong` ... in `src/agrona/jdk.ts` | Additions: JDK behaviour that a `long` held in a `number` or a `bigint` needs. `Integer.parseInt` / `Long.parseLong` accept only ASCII digits, whereas Java's `Character.digit` also takes other Unicode decimal digits (issue #17). |
| `AbstractMutableDirectBuffer` fields `byteArray`, `addressOffset`, `capacity`, and `UnsafeBuffer.wrapAdjustment` | `_byteArray`, `_addressOffset`, `_capacity`, `_wrapAdjustment` (new: `_dataView`) | A field cannot have the name of the accessor method of the same name. |
| `DirectBuffer.getLong` and the other `long` accessors / `MutableDirectBuffer.putLong` | add `getLongBigInt(index, byteOrder?)`, `putLongBigInt(index, value: bigint, byteOrder?)` | Additions (spec 5.2): an opaque 64-bit value. `getLong` throws `RangeError` for a stored value outside +/-(2^53 - 1), `putLong` and every `long` write (`putLongAscii`, `getAndAddLong`, ...) throw `RangeError`, writing nothing, for a result that is not a safe integer, where Java wraps. |
| Interface constants (`DirectBuffer.STR_HEADER_LEN`, `SHOULD_BOUNDS_CHECK`, `AtomicBuffer.ALIGNMENT`, ...) | `const DirectBuffer = {...}`, `const AtomicBuffer = {...}` beside the interface of the same name | A TypeScript interface cannot hold values. |
| `DirectBuffer.wrap` (7 overloads), `UnsafeBuffer` constructors (8) | `wrap(buffer: Uint8Array \| ArrayBuffer \| DirectBuffer, offset?, length?)`, `constructor(buffer?, offset?, length?)` | Overloads that differ in arity or only in the buffer type are one method over a union. |
| `getBytes` (5 overloads), `putBytes` (5), `getStringAscii` (6), `getStringWithoutLengthAscii` (2), `getStringUtf8` (3), `putStringUtf8` (4) | TypeScript overload signatures over one implementation, with the same names | The overloads differ by the type of an argument (`byte[]` / `MutableDirectBuffer`, `ByteOrder` / `int` / `Appendable`), so the call sites and the tests read as in Java. |
| `putStringAscii` (4 overloads), `putStringWithoutLengthAscii` (4) | `putStringAscii(index, value, byteOrder?)`, `putStringWithoutLengthAscii(index, value, valueOffset?, length?)` | `String` and `CharSequence` are both `string`; the rest differs in arity. |
| `getDigit(int, byte)` / `getDigit(int, char)` messages | one `getDigit(index, value)`: a negative `value` is a byte, which prints as the sign-extended char (`-56` is `'\uFFC8'`), a non-negative one is a char | Java's byte overload prints `(char) value`; the two are told apart by sign, so one method keeps both messages. |
| (none) | `LongOverflowError extends RangeError` with `readonly value: bigint` in `jdk.ts` | Addition (spec D5): thrown by `parseLongAscii`, `parseNaturalLongAscii`, `parseSize`, `parseDuration` and `getLong` for a long outside +/-(2^53 - 1), carrying the exact long Java returns. |
| (none) | `AsciiEncoding.isEightDigitAsciiEncodedNumberWords(high, low)`, `parseEightDigitsLittleEndianWords(high, low)` | Additions: the 8-digit helpers over two int32 words, so that the parsers allocate no `bigint`. |
| `Hashing.hash(long)`, `hash(long, int)`, `evenHash(long, int)`, `hash(Object, int)` | `hashLong`, `hashLong(value, mask)`, `evenHashLong`, `hashObject` | `int` and `long` are both `number`; the `Long` suffix rule (see `BitUtil`). `hash(int)` and `hash(int, int)` are one `hash(value, mask?)`. |
| `ArrayUtil.remove(T[], int)`, `ArrayListUtil.fastUnorderedRemove(ArrayList<T>, T)` | `ArrayUtil.removeAt`, `ArrayListUtil.fastUnorderedRemoveElement` | An element of type `number` and an index cannot be told apart. `ArrayListUtil.fastUnorderedRemove(list, index)` and `(list, index, lastIndex)` are one method with an optional argument. |
| `Int2ObjectHashMap.KeySet`, `ValueCollection`, `EntrySet`, `KeyIterator`, `ValueIterator`, `EntryIterator`, `EntryIterator.MapEntry` (the same in `Object2ObjectHashMap`, `Long2ObjectHashMap`, `Long2LongHashMap`) | `Int2ObjectHashMapKeySet`, `Int2ObjectHashMapValueCollection`, `Int2ObjectHashMapEntrySet`, `Int2ObjectHashMapKeyIterator`, `Int2ObjectHashMapValueIterator`, `Int2ObjectHashMapEntryIterator`, `Int2ObjectHashMapMapEntry` | A nested class is a top-level export of the same file named by the outer class and the nested name. Java inner classes read the private fields of the outer map; the fields they use are public with `@internal` and an underscore (`_keys`, `_values`, `_entries`, `_size`, `_shouldAvoidAllocation`), the others (`compactChain`, `reset`, `findNext`, `allocateDuplicateEntry`, `_mapNullValue`, `_unmapNullValue`, `_getMapped`) are public with `@internal`. |
| `Int2ObjectHashMap` fields `keys`, `values`, `size`, `loadFactor`, `resizeThreshold`, `valueCollection`, `keySet`, `entrySet` (the same in the other maps) | `_keys`, `_values`, `_size`, `_loadFactor`, `_resizeThreshold`, `valueCollection`, `_keySet`, `_entrySet` | A field cannot have the name of the accessor method of the same name. |
| `Int2ObjectHashMap.forEachInt`, `intForEach`, `replaceAllInt`, `KeySet.removeIfInt`, `EntrySet.removeIfInt`, `KeyIterator.nextInt`, `EntryIterator.getIntKey` | the same in `Int2ObjectHashMap`; `forEachLong`, `longForEach`, `replaceAllLong`, `removeIfLong`, `nextLong`, `getLongKey` in `Long2ObjectHashMap`; `Long2LongHashMap` has `forEachLong`, `longForEach`, `replaceAllLong`, `removeIfLong`, `nextValue`, `getLongKey`, `getLongValue` | What Agrona's generator makes of the `Int` names. |
| `java.util.function` `IntFunction`, `IntPredicate`, `LongPredicate`, `BiFunction`, `Function`, `IntUnaryOperator`, `IntBinaryOperator`, and `IntObjConsumer`, `IntObjPredicate`, `IntObjectToObjectFunction`, `LongLongConsumer`, ... | TS function types | A functional interface is a function type; a lambda argument is passed as is. |
| `Map.get`, `put`, ... returning `V` | return `V \| null` | A Java reference may be null. A `null` value argument is accepted by the type so that `NullPointerException("value cannot be null")` can be thrown, as in Java. |
| `Collection` / `Set` views and their `Iterator` | `AbstractCollection` / `AbstractSet` / `JIterator` of `jdkUtil.ts`, with `[Symbol.iterator]` | A `for ... of` over a view iterates with `iterator()` (the same cached iterator Java's for-each uses). `iterator()` keeps Java's `hasNext()` / `next()` / `remove()`. |
| `CountersReader.forEach(CounterConsumer)`, `forEach(MetaData)` (and `forEach(IntObjConsumer<String>)`) | `forEachCounterConsumer`, `forEachMetaData` (and `forEach`) | Overloads of one arity that differ only in the functional-interface type cannot be told apart at run time. |
| `Agent`, `IdleStrategy`, `CountersReader.MetaData` and the other `Consumer`-like functional interfaces | function types, except `NanoClock`, `EpochClock`, `ErrorHandler`, `Agent`, `IdleStrategy` (interfaces that classes implement) | A lambda is a function. |
| `AgentRunner`, `AgentInvoker`, `AtomicCounter`, ... private fields named like their accessor (`isClosed`, `isRunning`, `agent`, `thread`, `id`) | `_isClosed`, `_isRunning`, `_agent`, `_thread`, `_id` | A field cannot have the name of a method. |
| `BackoffIdleStrategy`, `SleepingIdleStrategy`, `NoOpIdleStrategy`, `YieldingIdleStrategy` `idle(int)` / `idle()` returning `void` | `idle(workCount?): number` returning the wait in ns | Spec D11. |
| `CloseHelper.close(ErrorHandler, AutoCloseable)`, `closeAll(ErrorHandler, ...)` | same names, one function with the plain overloads | See the `CloseHelper` source row. |
| (none) | `AgentRunnerScheduler`, `NODE_SCHEDULER`, `AgentRunner.wake()` in `AgentRunner.ts`; `Thread.switchTo`, `Thread.setAlive`, `Charset.encode` / `decode` in `jdkLang.ts` | Additions (spec D8, D11). |
| `ChannelUriStringBuilder` fields (`prefix`, `media`, `endpoint`, ...) and `ChannelUri` fields `prefix`, `media` | `_prefix`, `_media`, `_endpoint`, ... | A field cannot have the name of the method of the same name. |
| `ChannelUriStringBuilder.sessionId(Integer)` / `sessionId(String)` / `sessionId(ChannelUri)` / `sessionId()`, and the other getter / setter / `ChannelUri` overload sets | one method per name with overload signatures | Overloads that differ in the argument type or in arity become one method (spec 5.1). `Long` and `Integer` are both `number`: `nakDelay(String)` and `nakDelay(Long)` and `responseCorrelationId(Long)` and `(String)` are told apart by `typeof`. `tags(String)` and `tags(Long, Long)` are one method. |
| `ChannelUri.parse(CharSequence)` | `parse(uri: string)` | `CharSequence` is `string`. |
| `Header` fields `context`, `positionBitsToShift`, `initialTermId`, `offset`, `buffer`, `fragmentedFrameLength`; `BufferClaim.buffer` | `_context`, `_positionBitsToShift`, `_initialTermId`, `_offset`, `_buffer`, `_fragmentedFrameLength`; `_buffer` | A field cannot share a name with an accessor method. |
| `LogBufferDescriptor.packTail`, `rawTail`, `rawTailVolatile`, `casRawTail`, `termId(long)`, `termOffset(long)` with `long` rawTail | the same names with `bigint` | Spec D12. |
| `HeaderWriter.versionFlagsType`, `sessionId`, `streamId` (`long`, package-private) | `int` (`number`), public readonly | See the `HeaderWriter` source row. |
| (none) | `LogBufferDescriptor.rawTailTermId`, `rawTailTermOffset`; `FrameDescriptor` and `LogBufferDescriptor` are classes of static members | Additions (spec D12): the two halves of a tail slot without a `bigint`. |
| (none) | `LogBufferDescriptor.getAndAddRawTailOffset`, `MAX_POSITION`, `maxPossiblePosition` | Additions (spec D12, D13): the raw tail add without a `bigint`, and the position cap that Java computes inline in `Publication`. |
| (none) | `Hashing.hashLongHalves`, `evenHashLongHalves`; `LongHalves2ObjectHashMap`, `LongHalves2LongHashMap` and their nested classes; `receiverIdHigh`, `receiverIdLow` on `StatusMessageFlyweight`, `RttMeasurementFlyweight`, `ErrorFlyweight` | Additions (spec D14): 64-bit keys as `(hi, lo)` `int` halves. |
| `BufferBuilder.completeHeader` (package-private field) | `_completeHeader` | A field cannot have the name of the method `completeHeader(Header)`; it stays public for the tests. |
| `FragmentAssembler.delegate` / `isDirectByteBuffer` (fields) | `_delegate`, `_isDirectByteBuffer` | The accessors have the names. Same in `ControlledFragmentAssembler`, `ImageFragmentAssembler` and `ImageControlledFragmentAssembler`. |
| `BufferBuilder.buffer` (field) | `_buffer` | A field cannot have the name of the method `buffer()`. |
| `BufferBuilder.limit` / `nextTermOffset` (field and accessors) | `_limit`, `_nextTermOffset`; `limit(limit?)`, `nextTermOffset(offset?)` | The getter and the setter are one method; the fields carry the underscore, as in `Header`. |
| `TermReader.read(...)` with 8 arguments (returns the fragments read) and with 6 (returns the packed `long`); `TermReader.pack`, `fragmentsRead`, `offset`; `TermScanner.pack`, `available`, `padding` | one `read` told apart by `undefined !== subscriberPosition`; the packed outcomes are `number`s | Spec D13. |
| `TermGapScanner.GapHandler`, `TermUnblocker.Status` | `GapHandler` exported from `TermGapScanner.ts`; `Status` exported from `TermUnblocker.ts` and `TermUnblocker.Status` | A nested type is a sibling export; the enum is a class with the ordinals of Java. |
| `RetransmitHandler.RetransmitAction`, `RetransmitHandler.RetransmitAction.State` | `RetransmitAction` exported from `RetransmitHandler.ts`; `State` is the module constant of that file with the ordinals of Java | A nested class is a sibling export; the enum is a constant object of `number`s. |
| `IntHashSet.IntIterator`, `LongHashSet.LongIterator` | `IntHashSetIntIterator`, `LongHashSetLongIterator` | A nested class is a top-level export named by the outer class and the nested name (as the map iterators). |
| `DistinctErrorLog.DistinctObservation` (a record) | `DistinctObservation` | Top-level export of `DistinctErrorLog.ts`; the record accessors `throwable()` and `offset()` are methods. |
| `Throwable.printStackTrace(PrintStream / PrintWriter)`, `Throwable.toString()`, `System.err` | `printStackTrace(error, out)`, `stackTraceString(error)`, `throwableToString(error)`, `SystemErr` in `src/agrona/jdkThrowable.ts` | A JS `Error` has no such methods (spec D10, D18). |
| `idle(int)` / `idle()` of `BusySpinIdleStrategy`, `SleepingMillisIdleStrategy`, `ControllableIdleStrategy` returning `void` | `idle(workCount?): number` returning the wait in ns | Spec D11, as the phase 1 strategies. |
| `IntHashSet.removeIfInt(IntPredicate)`, `forEachInt(IntConsumer)` | the same; `removeIfLong`, `forEachLong` in `LongHashSet` | What Agrona's generator makes of the `Int` names. |
| `record NamedInterface(name, port)`, `InterfaceSearchAddress(address, subnetPrefix)`, `ResolvedInterface(localInterface, address)` accessors `name()`, `port()`, `address()`, `subnetPrefix()`, `localInterface()` | `readonly` fields `name`, `port`, `address`, `subnetPrefix`, `localInterface` | A record's accessor has the name of its component; a field of that name reads the same. `equals`, `hashCode` and `toString` are written out for `NamedInterface` and `InterfaceSearchAddress`. |
| `NetworkUtil.filterBySubnet` (2 overloads), `findAddressOnInterface` (2) | one function each, told apart by the type of an argument | See the `NetworkUtil` source row. |
| `Enumeration<NetworkInterface>` (`NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces`, `NetworkInterfaceShim.getNetworkInterfaces`), `List<InterfaceAddress>` | `NetworkInterface[]`, `InterfaceAddress[]` | Nothing else needs an `Enumeration`. |
| `NetworkInterfaceShim.DEFAULT`, `UnresolvedInterface.parse` (interface members) | `const NetworkInterfaceShim = { DEFAULT }`, `const UnresolvedInterface = { parse }` beside the interface of the same name | Interface constants and static methods, as `DirectBuffer.STR_HEADER_LEN`. |
| `SocketAddressParser.IpV4State`, `IpV6State` (enums) | string-literal union types `'HOST' \| 'PORT'` and `'START_ADDR' \| ... \| 'PORT'` | Private states of one function; a `switch` over the literals is as exhaustive. |
| `NameResolver.lookup`, `name`, `NameResolverAgent.init`, `doWork`, `roleName` (default methods) | required members, written by each implementer | A TypeScript interface has no default methods. |
| `UdpChannel.Context` (nested class) and its package-private fields | `UdpChannelContext` (top-level export of `UdpChannel.ts`); fields `controlMode_`, `remoteData_`, ... | A TypeScript class cannot have a field and a method of one name, and the fluent setters keep the Java names (`controlMode(ControlMode)`), so the fields get a trailing `_`. |
| `UdpChannel` private fields (`controlMode`, `remoteData`, `canonicalForm`, ...) and the accessors of the same names | fields `_controlMode`, `_remoteData`, `_canonicalForm`, ... | Same reason: the accessors `controlMode()`, `remoteData()`, `canonicalForm()` keep the Java names. |
| The getter and the fluent setter of a command flyweight field (`clientId()`, `clientId(long)`, `errorCode()`, `errorCode(ErrorCode)`, `channel()`, `channel(String)`, `sourceAddress()`, `sourceAddress(InetSocketAddress)`, ...) | one method with two overload signatures | The overloads differ only in arity (spec 5.1). |
| `RemovePublicationFlyweight.revoke()`, `revoke(int)`, `revoke(boolean)` | one overloaded `revoke(arg?)` | Told apart by the type of the argument (`number` or `boolean`). |
| `ReadableCounter` constructors `(CountersReader, long, int)` and `(CountersReader, int)` | one constructor with two overload signatures | Told apart by the number of arguments. |
| `ReadableCounter` fields `registrationId`, `counterId`, `isClosed`; `PublicationErrorFrame` fields `registrationId`, `sessionId`, ... | `_registrationId`, `_counterId`, `_isClosed`; `_registrationId`, `_sessionId`, ... | Same reason as `UdpChannel`: the accessors keep the Java names. |
| `CorrelatedMessageFlyweight.buffer`, `offset` (package-private) | `protected` | The flyweights that extend it are in the same package in Java. |
| `CorrelatedMessageFlyweight.CORRELATION_ID_FIELD_OFFSET`, `RemoveMessageFlyweight.REGISTRATION_ID_FIELD_OFFSET`, `TerminateDriverFlyweight.TOKEN_BUFFER_OFFSET` (package-private) | `public static readonly` | TypeScript has no package-private; the test and the sibling classes read them. |
| `CncFileDescriptor` static initializer block | `static readonly` initializers in layout order | `ERROR_LOG_BUFFER_LENGTH_FIELD_OFFSET` is declared before `CLIENT_LIVENESS_TIMEOUT_FIELD_OFFSET`, because the block assigns it first and a static initializer reads only what is above it. |
| `CncFileDescriptor.createMetaDataBuffer(ByteBuffer)` and the other `create*Buffer(ByteBuffer, DirectBuffer)` | `create*(buffer: Uint8Array \| ArrayBufferLike, ...)` | The CnC is a `SharedArrayBuffer` (spec D4, D15, D9). |
| `UdpChannel.parse` (3 overloads) | one `parse(channelUriString, nameResolver = DefaultNameResolver.INSTANCE, isDestination = false)` | Default parameters instead of overloads. |
| `UdpChannelTransport` constructors (2), `receive(ByteBuffer)`, `DatagramChannel` fields, `udpChannel()`, `multicastTtl()`, `receiveDatagramChannel()` | one constructor with optional buffer lengths; `receive(UnsafeBuffer)` with `receivedLength()`; `dgram.Socket` fields; the same method names, with the fields `_udpChannel`, `_multicastTtl` and `_receiveDatagramChannel` | See the `UdpChannelTransport` row of **Sources** and spec D28. |
| `WildcardPortManager` package-private fields (`portSet`, `highPort`, `lowPort`, `isOsWildcard`, `isSender`, `nextPort`) | public, `@internal` | TypeScript has no package-private; the extra test reads them. |
| `Integer.parseUnsignedInt(String)` | `Integer.parseUnsignedInt(s)` in `src/agrona/jdk.ts` | Addition (T12): decimal digits holding 0 to 2^32 - 1 as the `int` with those bits; `WildcardPortManager.parsePortRange` uses it. |
| `java.net.BindException`, `java.net.PortUnreachableException` | `BindException`, `PortUnreachableException` in `src/driver/jdkNet.ts` | Additions (spec D20), both `SocketException`s. |
| (none) | `SystemCounterDescriptor.RECEIVE_RING_DROPS`, `AeronCounters.SYSTEM_COUNTER_ID_RECEIVE_RING_DROPS` | Additions (spec D1, D29): the counter of the datagrams a full `ReceiveRing` dropped. The expected tables of `DriverStatusExpected.ts` list it. |
| `ControlMode` (enum), `InvalidChannelException` (3 constructors) | class with `static readonly` constants and `name()`, `ordinal()`, `values()`; one constructor with overload signatures | See their source rows; same as `ErrorCode` and `Category`. |
| `Long.parseLong(String)`, `Long.valueOf(String)` | `Long.parseLong(s)` in `src/agrona/jdk.ts` | Addition (spec D5): a decimal `long` as a `number`; a value inside Java's `long` but outside +/-(2^53 - 1) throws `LongOverflowError`, a malformed or 64-bit-overflowing one `NumberFormatException` with Java's message. Used by `UdpChannel` for `tags` and `gtag`. |
| (none) | `DefaultNameResolver.cache(name, address)`, `clearCache()`; `InetAddress.nameCache` | Additions (spec D7, D20): the host-name table that stands in for the system resolver; the client's `async*` add calls fill it after `dns.lookup`. |
| `SystemCounterDescriptor` (enum) and `EnumMap<SystemCounterDescriptor, AtomicCounter>` in `SystemCounters` | `SystemCounterDescriptor` (a class with `static readonly` instances) and `AtomicCounter[]` indexed by `descriptor.ordinal()` | An `EnumMap` is an array indexed by ordinal (that is how the JDK implements it), so the array keeps Java's lookup cost (one index) and iteration order. `SystemCounterDescriptor.get(int)` (the static lookup by id) keeps its name; `SystemCounters.get(descriptor)` is the instance method. |
| `SessionKey(int, String)` and `SessionKey(int, int, String)` | `SessionKey(streamId, channel)` and `SessionKey(sessionId, streamId, channel)`, two overload signatures of one constructor | Told apart by the number of arguments. |
| `SendChannelEndpoint.send(ByteBuffer)`, `send(ByteBuffer, InetSocketAddress)`; `ReceiveChannelEndpoint.sendTo(ByteBuffer, InetSocketAddress)` | `send(buffer, offset, length)`, `sendTo(buffer, offset, length, address)` | Overrides of the transport's methods (spec D28, D32); a `ByteBuffer` is an `UnsafeBuffer` window. |
| `ReceiveChannelEndpoint.send(ByteBuffer, int, ImageConnection[])`, `send(ByteBuffer, int, int, InetSocketAddress)` | `sendToImageConnections(buffer, length, connections)`, `sendToSource(buffer, length, transportIndex, address)` | The transport has a `send` already (spec D32). |
| `MultiSndDestination.send(DatagramChannel, ByteBuffer, SendChannelEndpoint, int)` and the static `send(DatagramChannel, ByteBuffer, SendChannelEndpoint, int, int, InetSocketAddress, ErrorHandler)` | `send(channelEndpoint, buffer, offset, bytesToSend)` and the static `send(channelEndpoint, buffer, bytesToSend, position, destination)` | The channel is the socket of the endpoint, the errors are the transport's (spec D32). |
| `MultiSndDestination.removeDestination(long)`; `destinationsCounter(AtomicCounter)` with the field `destinationsCounter` | `removeDestinationById`; the method keeps the name and the field is `_destinationsCounter` | A class cannot have a method and a field of one name; `removeDestination(ChannelUri, InetSocketAddress)` and `removeDestination(long)` are told apart by the argument type on `SendChannelEndpoint`, and by name on the abstract class. |
| `Destination.receiverId` (`long`) | `receiverIdHigh`, `receiverIdLow`, and `receiverId()` (a `bigint`) | Spec D33. `isMatch(receiverIdHigh, receiverIdLow, address)`. |
| `ReceiveChannelEndpoint.isMulticast()` / `isMulticast(int)`, `udpChannel()` / `udpChannel(int)`, `multicastTtl()` / `multicastTtl(int)`, `destination(UdpChannel)` / `destination(int)`, `sendRttMeasurement` (two forms), `MultiRcvDestination.transport(int)` / `transport(UdpChannel)`, `SendChannelEndpoint.removeDestination` (two forms), `ReceiveDestinationTransport.timeOfLastActivityNs()` / `(long)` and `currentControlAddress()` / `(InetSocketAddress)` | one method each: optional index (default 0), a union of the two argument types, or a getter and a setter told apart by the number of arguments | Overloads that differ in arity or in the type of one argument are one method (spec 5.1). |
| `ReceiveChannelEndpoint` fields `dispatcher`, `localSocketAddressIndicator`; `SendChannelEndpoint` field `localSocketAddressIndicator`; `MultiSndDestination` field `destinationsCounter` | `_dispatcher`, `_localSocketAddressIndicator`, `_destinationsCounter` | The accessors have the Java names (same rule as `UdpChannel`). |
| The package-private members read by the tests (`MultiSndDestination.destinations`, `roundRobinIndex`, the `Destination` fields, `ReceiveChannelEndpoint.timeOfLastActivityNs`, `checkForReResolution`) | public, `@internal` | TypeScript has no package-private; the tests are in another directory. |
| `java.nio.channels.ClosedChannelException` | `ClosedChannelException` in `src/driver/jdkNet.ts` | Addition (spec D20). |
| `Int2IntCounterMap`, `Long2LongCounterMap` (generated) | `Int2IntCounterMap`, `LongHalves2LongCounterMap` (keyed by two `int` halves) | Spec D14, D34. |
| `DataPacketDispatcher.addSubscription(int)` / `(int, int)`, `removeSubscription(int)` / `(int, int)` | `addSubscription(streamId, sessionId?)`, `removeSubscription(streamId, sessionId?)` | Overloads that differ in arity are one method (spec 5.1). |
| `DataPacketDispatcher.SessionState` (enum), `StreamInterest` (static nested class) | `SessionState`, `StreamInterest`, exported from `DataPacketDispatcher.ts` | A nested class is a top-level export; an enum is a class with `static readonly` constants (as `ControlMode`). |
| `PendingSetupMessageFromSource` fields `sessionId`, `streamId`, `transportIndex`, `channelEndpoint`, `controlAddress`, `timeOfStatusMessageNs` | `_sessionId`, `_streamId`, `_transportIndex`, `_channelEndpoint`, `_controlAddress`, `_timeOfStatusMessageNs` | The accessors have the Java names. |
| `SubscriptionLink.matches(NetworkPublication)`, `matches(PublicationImage)`, `matches(IpcPublication)`, `matches(ReceiveChannelEndpoint, int, SubscriptionParams)`, `matches(ReceiveChannelEndpoint, int, int)` | `matchesNetworkPublication`, `matchesImage`, `matchesIpcPublication`, `matchesParams`, `matchesSession` | The overloads differ by the type of the first argument, which TypeScript cannot tell apart at run time for interfaces (spec D43). |
| the fields of `SubscriptionLink` that have an accessor (`registrationId`, `streamId`, `sessionId`, `hasSessionId`, `isSparse`, `isTether`, `isResponse`, `isRejoin`, `group`, `channel`, `aeronClient`) and `AeronClient.clientId` | `_registrationId`, `_streamId`, ... and `_clientId` | A class cannot have a field and a method of one name. Java's `DriverConductor` reads some of these fields directly; T18 uses the accessors. |
| `UntetheredSubscription.state` (field) and `state(State, long, int, int)` | `state()` and `state(newState, nowNs, streamId, sessionId)`, one overloaded method | As above. |
| `UntetheredSubscription.State`, `NetworkSubscriptionLink.ResponseSetupState` (nested enums) | `UntetheredSubscriptionState`, `ResponseSetupState` (classes with `values`, `name`, `ordinal`), also static members `UntetheredSubscription.State` and `NetworkSubscriptionLink.ResponseSetupState` | A nested type is a top-level export with a static member of the outer class, as `AeronException.Category`. |
| `DriverConductorProxy.driverConductor` (field) | `_driverConductor` | The method `driverConductor(conductor)` has the Java name. |
| `DriverConductorProxy.createPublicationImage` and the conductor's `onCreatePublicationImage` | unchanged | Listed because the placeholder of T13 had `createPublicationImage` only; the real class has all nine members. |
| `PublicationImage.State` (nested enum) | `State` (const object of ordinals) and `STATE_NAMES` | As `NetworkPublication.State`; the name is for `toString`. |
| `PublicationImage` fields that share a name with a method | a leading `_` | As the other classes. |
| `ReceiverProxy.receiver(Receiver)` | `receiver()` getter and setter | One member. |
| `LossReport.ReportEntry` (nested class) | `ReportEntry`, also `LossReport.ReportEntry` | A nested class is a top-level export. |
| `Publication.offer(DirectBuffer)`, `offer(DirectBuffer, int, int)`, `offer(DirectBuffer, int, int, ReservedValueSupplier)` | `offer(buffer, offset?, length?, reservedValueSupplier?)` | Overloads that differ in arity are one method (spec 5.1). |
| `Publication.offer(DirectBuffer, int, int, DirectBuffer, int, int[, ReservedValueSupplier])`, `offer(DirectBufferVector[][, ReservedValueSupplier])` | `offerTwoBuffers`, `offerVectored` | The overloads cannot be told apart by arity and type at run time (spec 5.1, D80). |
| `ConcurrentPublication` / `ExclusivePublication` private `appendUnfragmentedMessage`, `appendFragmentedMessage` (3 overloads each) | `appendUnfragmentedMessage`, `appendFragmentedMessage`, `...TwoBuffers`, `...Vectored` | As `offer`. |
| `ExclusivePublication.appendPadding(UnsafeBuffer, int, int)` (private) | `appendPaddingFrame` | The public `appendPadding(int)` has the name. |
| `Publication.removeDestination(String)`, `(long)`, `asyncRemoveDestination(String)`, `(long)` | `removeDestination(string \| number)`, `asyncRemoveDestination(string \| number)` | A `string` and a `number` are told apart at run time by the conductor. |
| `Subscription.channelStatusId()` and the package-private `channelStatusId(int)` | `channelStatusId()` and `channelStatusId(id)`, one overloaded method | A getter and a setter of one name. |
| `Publication`, `ExclusivePublication`, `Subscription` fields that share a name with a method (`isClosed`, `revokeOnClose`, `streamId`, `sessionId`, `registrationId`, `originalRegistrationId`, `channel`, `positionLimit`, `termBufferLength`, `initialTermId`, `maxMessageLength`, `maxPayloadLength`, `positionBitsToShift`, `maxPossiblePosition`, `channelStatusId`, `termId`, `termOffset`, `images`) and `Image` fields of the same kind | a leading `_` | As the other classes. |
| `Publication.logBuffers`, `isClosed`, `revokeOnClose` fields read by `ClientConductor` | `logBuffers()`, `_isClosed`, `_revokeOnClose` | T19 reads the fields with these names. |
| `Image` fields that share a name with a method (`correlationId`, `joinPosition`, `sessionId`, `initialTermId`, `positionBitsToShift`, `isClosed`, `logBuffers`, `sourceIdentity`, `subscription`) | a leading `_` | As the other classes. |
| `Subscription.images()` (`List<Image>`) | `images()` answering a `readonly Image[]` | The held array, a snapshot (spec D80). |
| `Subscription.forEachImage(Consumer<Image>)` | `forEachImage((image) => void)` | A lambda is a function. |
| `Aeron.Context`, `Aeron.Configuration` | `AeronContext`, `AeronConfiguration` (exported from `src/client/Aeron.ts`), also `Aeron.Context` and `Aeron.Configuration` for the value and the type | A nested class is a top-level export with a static member on the outer class, as `MediaDriver.Context` (spec D90). |
| `ClientConductor.PendingSubscription` | `PendingSubscription` (exported from `src/client/ClientConductor.ts`), also `ClientConductor.PendingSubscription` | The same. |
| the fields of `Aeron.Context` (`clientName`, `errorHandler`, ...) | `_clientName`, `_errorHandler`, ... | As the fields of `MediaDriver.Context`. |
| `Aeron.NULL_VALUE` read by the files that `Aeron.ts` imports | `NULL_VALUE` of `src/client/NullValue.ts` | The import cycle (spec D90). `Aeron.NULL_VALUE` is the same value. |
| `MappedLogBuffersFactory` | `MemoryLogBuffersFactory` | The logs are in memory (spec D91). |
| `ClientConductor.removePublication(Publication)` / `(long)`, `removeSubscription(Subscription)` / `(long)` | `removePublication(publication \| registrationId)`, `removeSubscription(subscription \| registrationId)` | A `Publication` and a number are told apart at run time (spec D96). |
| `ClientConductor.removeDestination(long, String)` / `(long, long)`, `asyncRemoveDestination(long, String)` / `(long, long)`, `DriverProxy.removeDestination(long, String)` / `(long, long)` | `removeDestination(registrationId, destination: string \| number)`, `asyncRemoveDestination(...)` | The same, as `Publication.removeDestination` (spec D80). |
| `ClientConductor.removeCloseHandler(long)` / `(Runnable)`, `Aeron.removeCloseHandler` | `removeCloseHandler(registrationId \| handler)` | The same. |
| `ClientConductor.addSubscription(String, int)` / `(String, int, AvailableImageHandler, UnavailableImageHandler)` and `asyncAddSubscription` | `addSubscription(channel, streamId, availableImageHandler?, unavailableImageHandler?)` | Overloads that differ in arity are one method (spec 5.1); an omitted handler (`undefined`) is the default of the context, a `null` handler is none. |
| `DriverProxy.addPublication(String, int)` and the other commands with a channel, `removePublication` and `removeSubscription` | an optional last parameter `correlationId` | Added (spec D92). |
| `Runnable` (close handler) | `() => void` | A Java functional interface that is a lambda (spec D83). |
| `ErrorHandler`-typed `DEFAULT_ERROR_HANDLER` that exits the process on a `DriverTimeoutException` | prints and does not exit | A library does not exit its process (spec D93). |

## Not ported

Members of ported files left out because they serve only out-of-scope features (spec §3).

| Java member | Reason |
|---|---|
| `SystemUtil.PID_NOT_FOUND`, `getPid`, `osName`, `osArch`, `isWindows`, `isLinux`, `isMac`, `isX64Arch()` (the no-argument one), `isDebuggerAttached`, `tmpDirName`, `threadDump` (2 overloads) | OS, JVM, process and file-system queries, with no JavaScript equivalent that Aeron's transport uses (spec 3). |
| `SystemUtil.NULL_PROPERTY_VALUE`, `getProperty` (2), `loadPropertiesFile` (2), `loadPropertiesFiles` (2), `loadProperties`, `PropertyAction` | Properties files and the `@null` convention of Agrona's `getProperty`. The system properties themselves are `System` of `jdkLang.ts` (spec D22), and `getSizeAsInt`, `getSizeAsLong` and `getDurationInNanos`, which read them, are ported (T7b). |
| `ExpandableDirectByteBuffer` (the class) and `ExpandableArrayBuffer.byteBuffer()` | No `ByteBuffer` and no direct memory (D9); the rows of `MutableDirectBufferTests` and `UnsafeBufferTest` that used `ExpandableDirectByteBuffer` are listed under **Adapted tests**. |
| `DirectBuffer.wrap(ByteBuffer)`, `wrap(ByteBuffer, int, int)`, `wrap(long, int)`, `byteBuffer()`, `getBytes(int, ByteBuffer, int)`, `getBytes(int, ByteBuffer, int, int)` | No `ByteBuffer` and no raw memory address in JS: an `ArrayBuffer` is wrapped as a `Uint8Array`. |
| `MutableDirectBuffer.putBytes(int, ByteBuffer, int)`, `putBytes(int, ByteBuffer, int, int)` | As above. |
| `AbstractMutableDirectBuffer` `getBytes` / `putBytes` with a `ByteBuffer`; the `reverseBytes` branches (`NATIVE_BYTE_ORDER != LITTLE_ENDIAN` is never true); the `IOException` catch around `Appendable.append` | As above; the native order is little-endian; `append` cannot throw a checked exception. |
| `AtomicBuffer.STRICT_ALIGNMENT_CHECKS_PROP_NAME`, `STRICT_ALIGNMENT_CHECKS` | JVM system property and `os.arch`; alignment of a `byte[]` is not observable. `verifyAlignment` checks the window offset only. |
| `UnsafeBuffer(ByteBuffer)`, `(ByteBuffer, int, int)`, `(long, int)`, `byteBuffer()`, the `byteBuffer` field | As above. |
| `BufferUtil.BYTE_BUFFER_HB_FIELD_OFFSET`, `BYTE_BUFFER_OFFSET_FIELD_OFFSET`, `BYTE_BUFFER_ADDRESS_FIELD_OFFSET`, `boundsCheck(ByteBuffer, long, int)`, `address`, `array`, `arrayOffset`, `free(DirectBuffer)`, `free(ByteBuffer)` | Methods on a `ByteBuffer` or its memory; memory is garbage collected. |
| `UnsafeApi.java` | The `sun.misc.Unsafe` / `VarHandle` wrapper: its calls in the buffers are `DataView` calls. |
| `Int2IntHashMap` (as a TS class), the generated `Long2NullableObjectHashMap`, `Object2NullableObjectHashMap`, `Int2ObjectCache`, `Object2IntHashMap`, `Int2IntCounterMap` and the other collections | Not used by the transport; `Long2LongHashMap` is ported from the `Int2IntHashMap` template directly. |
| `Long2LongHashMap` boxed `Map` methods whose result differs from the primitive one: `get(Object)`, `put(Long, Long)`, `putIfAbsent(Long, Long)`, `replace(Long, Long)`, `replace(Long, Long, Long)` (boxed overload), `remove(Object)` | They return null where the primitive ones return the missing value, and a `number` cannot tell the two overloads apart. Use the primitive methods and `containsKey`. |
| `Int2ObjectHashMapConformanceTest`, `Long2ObjectHashMapConformanceTest`, `Long2LongHashMapConformanceTest`, `Object2ObjectHashMapConformanceTest` (`org.agrona.collections`, the Guava testlib suites run by `ConformanceTestsSuite`) | Not ported: requires Guava testlib. |
| `java.util.Map` / `Collection` members the tests and the transport do not use: `Collection.stream`, `spliterator`, `retainAll`, `removeAll`, `addAll`, `Serializable` | No TS counterpart. The views implement `size`, `isEmpty`, `contains`, `containsAll`, `iterator`, `toArray`, `removeIf`, `forEach`, `clear`, `equals`, `hashCode`, `toString`. |
| `AgentRunner.startOnThread(AgentRunner, ThreadFactory)`, `Thread`-based `run()` blocking | There are no threads or thread factories; `startOnThread(runner)` starts the runner on the event loop. |
| `AbstractConcurrentArrayQueue.sharedHeadCache`, the padding classes, `BUFFER_ARRAY_BASE`, `SHIFT_FOR_SCALE`, `TAIL_OFFSET`, `HEAD_OFFSET`; `ManyToOneConcurrentArrayQueue`, `ManyToManyConcurrentArrayQueue`, `QueuedPipeTest` and the other queue tests | `Unsafe` layout and the many-producer queues serve other threads; Aeron's driver uses only `OneToOneConcurrentArrayQueue` (`CommandProxy`), which has extra tests in `test/extra/agrona/`. |
| `CountersReader`, `CountersManager`, `AtomicCounter`: the `byteBuffer()` / `byteArray()` / address fields | No `ByteBuffer` or address (spec D9). |
| `AtomicLongPosition` and `AtomicLongPositionTest` | Not used by the transport: the in-memory counters are single-threaded and the driver's positions are `UnsafeBufferPosition`. |
| `IntArrayList`: `wrap`, `trimToSize`, `get`, `add`, `add(int, Integer)`, `addInt(int, int)`, `set`, `setInt`, `contains`, `containsInt`, `indexOf`, `lastIndexOf`, `addAll`, `containsAll`, `retainAll`, `removeAll`, `removeIfInt`, `remove(Object)`, `fastUnorderedRemove`, `removeInt`, `fastUnorderedRemoveInt`, `popInt`, `forEachOrderedInt`, `intStream`, `toIntArray`, `ensureCapacity`, `equals`, `hashCode`, `forEach`, `forEachInt`, `checkIndexForAdd` | Not used by `CountersManager`; ported when a task needs them. |
| `CloseHelper` closeAll(null handler, ...) `NullPointerException` branch | See the `CloseHelper` source row: a `null` first argument cannot be told from a null closeable. |
| `Agent`/`AgentRunner`: `AgentRunner.TOMBSTONE` thread `Thread::new` creation, `ShutdownSignalBarrier`, `DynamicCompositeAgent`, `CountedErrorHandler`, `SnowflakeIdGenerator`, `Pipe`-using `ManyToOne*` queues | Not in the T5 list. |
| `Aeron.addCounter` (2), `addStaticCounter` (2), `asyncAddCounter` (2), `asyncAddStaticCounter` (2), `getCounter`, `asyncRemoveCounter`, `addAvailableCounterHandler`, `removeAvailableCounterHandler` (2), `addUnavailableCounterHandler`, `removeUnavailableCounterHandler` (2); `Aeron.Context.availableCounterHandler`, `unavailableCounterHandler`, `aeronDirectoryName`, `threadFactory`; `Aeron.Configuration.DEFAULT_ERROR_HANDLER`'s exit of the process; the `@Config` annotations | The counters API and the counter handlers (spec D95, D40, D54), the Aeron directory (spec D4), the threads of Java (spec D3) and the configuration tooling. `Aeron.printCounters` uses `println` and `toLocaleString` for `%3d: %,20d - %s%n`. |
| `ClientConductor.addCounter` (2), `addStaticCounter` (2), `asyncAddCounter` (2), `asyncAddStaticCounter` (2), `asyncRemoveCounter`, `getCounter`, `removeCounter`, `onNewCounter`, `onAvailableCounter`, `onUnavailableCounter`, `onStaticCounter`, `onNextAvailableSessionId`, the counter handler maps and `notifyCounterAvailable` / `notifyUnavailableCounterHandlers`; `DriverProxy.addCounter` (2), `addStaticCounter` (2), `removeCounter`, `nextAvailableSessionId`; `DriverEventsAdapter` for `ON_COUNTER_READY`, `ON_UNAVAILABLE_COUNTER`, `ON_STATIC_COUNTER`, `ON_NEXT_AVAILABLE_SESSION_ID`; `AvailableCounterHandler`, `UnavailableCounterHandler`, `Counter`, `CounterProvider` users; `CounterUpdateFlyweight`, `StaticCounterFlyweight`, `NextAvailableSessionIdFlyweight`, `CounterMessageFlyweight`, `StaticCounterMessageFlyweight`, `RemoveCounterFlyweight`, `GetNextAvailableSessionIdMessageFlyweight`; `RethrowingErrorHandler` | The counters API and `nextAvailableSessionId` are out of scope (spec D95, D40); `nextSessionId` answers a randomised id. |
| `LogBuffers(String, boolean readOnly)`, `LogBuffers.fileChannel()` as a `FileChannel`, the mapping of a log over `Integer.MAX_VALUE` in several mapped buffers | The logs are in memory (spec D4, D91). |
| `CommonContext.AERON_DIR_PROP_DEFAULT`, `getAeronDirectoryName`, `generateRandomDirName`, `newDefaultCncFile`, `newCncFile`, `aeronDirectoryName` (both), `aeronDirectory`, `cncFile`, `concludeAeronDirectory`, `deleteAeronDirectory`, `mapExistingCncFile`, `saveErrorLog` (2), the `isConcluded` var handle | Aeron directory and CnC file: files and shared memory (spec D4). The in-memory CnC is built by `MediaDriver.Context`. |
| `CommonContext.SECURE_RANDOM_ALGORITHM_DEFAULT`, `getSecureRandomAlgorithm` | The JVM's secure random algorithms. (`threadName` is ported.) |
| `CommonContext.isDriverActive` (2), `requestDriverTermination`, `printErrorLog`, `saveExistingErrors`, `errorLogBuffer`, `driverFilePageSize`, `nextCorrelationId`, `awaitCncFileCreation`, `sleep`, `DEBUG_FIELDS_SEEN` and the message of `checkDebugTimeout` | They need the driver, the CnC file or the filesystem; the message of `checkDebugTimeout` needs the name of the calling method. `driverFilePageSize` is `CncFileDescriptor.filePageSize`, which `Aeron.Context.conclude` calls. |
| `LogUtil.appendTimestamp`, `renderTimestamp` | Timestamp formatting for the event logs (they format seconds and zero-padded nanoseconds with `AsciiEncoding.digitCount`; there is no `java.time`); not used by the transport. `appendIpV6Address` is ported because `ResolutionEntryFlyweight` needs it. |
| `Configuration.sendChannelEndpointSupplier`, `receiveChannelEndpointSupplier`, `congestionControlSupplier`, `terminationValidator` | Their types are not ported yet; the task that ports each adds it (see the `Configuration` row of **Sources**: T13, T10, T18 and T12). |
| `CubicCongestionControlSupplier` (`io/aeron/driver/ext`) | Not ported: it only constructs `CubicCongestionControl`, which `DefaultCongestionControlSupplier` selects with `cc=cubic`. `MediaDriver.Context.congestionControlSupplier` and `unicastFlowControlSupplier` are added with the task that wires the suppliers into the Context (T13 and later). |
| `MediaDriver.Context`: the endpoint suppliers, flow control and congestion control suppliers, delay generators, transport pollers, the proxies and command queues, `ClientProxy`, `logFactory`, `terminationValidator`, `nativeResourceAgent*` | Their types are not ported yet; the task that ports each adds it (see the `MediaDriver` row of **Sources**). |
| `MediaDriver.Context`: the five `*ThreadFactory` members, `aeronDirectoryName`, `deleteDirectory`, `warnIfDirectoryExists`, `dirDeleteOnStart`, `dirDeleteOnShutdown`, `useWindowsHighResTimer`, `lossReport`, `lossReportBuffer`, the mark file, the `DriverNameResolver` branch | Threads (spec D3), the Aeron directory and files (D4), the Windows timer, the loss report file and the name resolver gossip (spec section 3). |
| `PrintBufferUtil.hexDump` (4 overloads), `appendHexDump` (4), `prettyHexDump` (2), `appendPrettyHexDump` (2), `NEWLINE`, `HexUtil` | Hex dump formatting; only `byteToHexStringPadded` is used. |
| `HeaderWriter(long, long, long)` constructor, `NativeBigEndianHeaderWriter` | The big-endian writer: the port is little-endian (spec 5.2 `NATIVE_BYTE_ORDER`), so `HeaderWriter.newInstance` always returns a `HeaderWriter`. The three-`long` constructor is only used by the subclass. |
| `FrameDescriptor.frameLengthVolatile` / `frameLengthOrdered`, `BufferClaim.commit` / `abort`: the `ByteOrder.nativeOrder() != LITTLE_ENDIAN` / `reverseBytes` branches | As above: never taken. |
| `BufferClaim(...)` use of `new UnsafeBuffer(long, int)` | No raw memory addresses (spec D9): an empty `UnsafeBuffer`. |
| `BufferBuilder.newDirectBuffer` returning a `ByteBuffer` (`allocateDirect`, `order(LITTLE_ENDIAN)`) | An `ArrayBuffer` (spec D9); `UnsafeBuffer` is little-endian only. |
| `ControlledAssemblyTest`, `PubAndSubTest` (the assembler cases) and the other system tests that use the assemblers | They need a media driver and a publication. The assemblers are covered by `FragmentAssemblerTest`, `BufferBuilderTest` and `test/extra/client/AssemblersExtraTest.test.ts` in phase 1, and the end-to-end cases belong to the later phases that have a client and a driver. |
| `ObjectHashSet` | Phase 2 plan decision 9: the conductors' object sets are JS `Set`s (control plane only). `LongHashSet` and `IntHashSet` are ported. |
| `OffsetEpochNanoClock`, `HighResolutionClock` | Not used by the transport; `SystemEpochNanoClock` reads `performance.timeOrigin` and `performance.now()` itself. |
| `ManyToOneConcurrentLinkedQueue` as a multi-thread queue | A JS object cannot be shared between workers; it is a single-thread queue (spec D19). Cross-thread hand-off uses the ring buffers (D16). |
| `Throwable.printStackTrace` details: the `... n more` elision of the frames a cause shares, `Suppressed:` blocks, `getStackTrace()` elements | Spec D18: the frames are V8's text, all printed; suppressed exceptions are not printed. |
| `NetworkUtil.allocateDirectAlignedAndPadded` | Returns a direct `ByteBuffer`; there are none (spec D9). Its caller, `UdpChannelTransport`, receives into the poller's preallocated buffer. |
| `UdpChannelTransport.sendError(int, IOException, InetSocketAddress)` | Deprecated for removal since 1.46.6 in favour of `onSendError`, which is ported. |
| The `DriverLog.logFrameOut` / `logFrameIn` / `logResend` calls of `UdpChannelTransport.sendHook` / `receiveHook` / `resendHook`, and the `Selector` of `TransportPoller` | The logging agents are out of scope (spec section 3), so the hooks exist and do nothing; a node socket cannot be selected (spec D30). |
| `TimeTrackingNameResolver.logHostName`, and the `DriverLog.logResolve` / `logLookup` calls in `resolve` and `lookup` | The driver logging agents are out of scope (spec section 3). |
| `UdpChannel`: `serialVersionUID` of `InvalidChannelException`; the `@SuppressWarnings` annotations; `Context.hasNoDistinguishingCharacteristic` is set and never read in Java, and is ported as set | Not applicable (no serialization or annotations); nothing is omitted from the behaviour. Every parameter is ported, with the multicast, MDC, tag, buffer, window, timestamp-offset, `gtag` and `nak-delay` parts that phase 3 uses; their tests beyond `UdpChannelTest` are in `test/extra/driver/media/UdpChannelExtraTest.test.ts`. |
| `CounterMessageFlyweight`, `CounterUpdateFlyweight`, `RemoveCounterFlyweight`, `StaticCounterFlyweight`, `StaticCounterMessageFlyweight` | Plan decision 8 and spec section 3: the counters API (`Aeron.addCounter`, `Counter`, static counters) is out of scope. `ControlProtocolEvents` keeps the counter constants (`ADD_COUNTER`, `REMOVE_COUNTER`, `ADD_STATIC_COUNTER`, `ON_COUNTER_READY`, `ON_UNAVAILABLE_COUNTER`, `ON_STATIC_COUNTER`). |
| `NextAvailableSessionIdFlyweight`, `GetNextAvailableSessionIdMessageFlyweight` | Spec section 3: `Aeron.nextAvailableSessionId` is out of scope. `ControlProtocolEvents` keeps `GET_NEXT_AVAILABLE_SESSION_ID` and `ON_NEXT_AVAILABLE_SESSION_ID`. |
| `CncFileDescriptor.isCncFile(Path, BasicFileAttributes)` | Matches a path name against `cnc.dat`; there is no CnC file (spec D4). `CNC_FILE` stays as a constant. |
| `DefaultNameResolver` resolution through the JVM resolver (`InetAddress.getByName` of a host name, with its DNS and `/etc/hosts` lookup), `InetAddress.getAllByName`, reverse lookups, `InetSocketAddress(String, int)` | Node's resolver is asynchronous; the host names are resolved by the client with `dns.lookup` and cached (spec D7). |
| `AeronCounters.validateCounterTypeId(Aeron, Counter, int)` | The `Counter` class and `Aeron.addCounter` are out of scope (plan decision 8), and so is the overload that takes them. The overload that takes a `CountersReader` is ported. |
| `DutyCycleTrackerLhsPadding`, `DutyCycleTrackerFields` and the `p000`..`p127` padding fields of `DutyCycleTracker` | Cache line padding against false sharing between the tracker's hot field and its neighbours on the JVM heap. A JavaScript object has no field layout to control and the tracker is owned by one agent (spec D11, D19). |
| The `@AeronCounter` annotation on the `AeronCounters` constants | It only drives the Java annotation processor that cross-checks the C counter names (`expectedCName`, `existsInC`); there is no C build in this repository. |
| The accessors `majorVersion()`, `minorVersion()`, `patchVersion()`, `gitSha()`, `toString()` and the `Version` interface that `VersionProcessor` adds to the generated `MediaDriverVersion` | Nothing in the port calls them; the constants are read directly. |
| The `*LhsPadding`, `*HotFields` and `*RhsPadding` classes and the `p000`..`p127` fields of `MultiSndDestination`, `Destination`, `ReceiveChannelEndpoint`, `ReceiveDestinationTransport` and `ImageConnection` | Cache line padding against false sharing on the JVM heap; a JS object has no field layout to control and each is owned by one agent (spec D11, D19). The hot fields stay where Java keeps them (`timeOfLastActivityNs` of `ReceiveChannelEndpoint` is a public field). |
| The `Selector`, `SelectionKey`, `Consumer<SelectionKey>` poller and the `ITERATION_THRESHOLD` branch of `ControlTransportPoller.pollTransports` and `DataTransportPoller.pollTransports`; the `selectNowWithoutProcessing()` after a cancel | A node socket cannot be selected (spec D30); a poller visits every transport in turn. |
| `DriverLog.logNakReceived` (`SendChannelEndpoint.onNakMessage`) and `DriverLog.logNaksSent` (`ReceiveChannelEndpoint.sendNakMessage`) | The logging agents are out of scope (spec section 3). |
| `ReceiveChannelEndpoint` field `channelReceiveTimestampClock` | Java reads `context.channelReceiveTimestampClock()` and never uses it; `PublicationImage` (T16) reads the clock from the context (the two int halves of `EpochNanoClock`, spec D17). |
| The `@Config` annotations of `DebugChannelEndpointConfiguration`, `BiInt2ObjectMap`, and the `Random` members other than the constructors and `nextDouble` | The annotations only drive Java's configuration documentation processor. `BiInt2ObjectMap` is not needed: the loss generators use `LongHalves2ObjectHashMap` (spec D60). Nothing else of `java.util.Random` is used (spec D61). |
| `ClientProxy.onCounterReady`, `onStaticCounter`, `onUnavailableCounter`, `onNextAvailableSessionId`, and the `ADD_COUNTER`, `REMOVE_COUNTER`, `ADD_STATIC_COUNTER` and `GET_NEXT_AVAILABLE_SESSION_ID` cases of `ClientCommandAdapter.onMessage` (with the five flyweight fields they use) | Plan decision 8 and spec section 3: the counters API and `nextAvailableSessionId` are out of scope, and their flyweights are not ported. The four commands get an `UNKNOWN_COMMAND_TYPE_ID` error response (spec D40). |
| `IpcSubscriptionLink`, `SpySubscriptionLink` | IPC and spy subscriptions are not ported (the 29 IPC and spy tests of `DriverConductorTest` are `it.skip`, plan T18). `SubscriptionLink` keeps `matchesNetworkPublication` and `matchesIpcPublication`, which only these two override. |
| `DriverLog.logCmd` (`ClientCommandAdapter`, `ClientProxy`) and `DriverLog.logUntetheredSubscriptionStateChange` (`UntetheredSubscription`) | The logging agents are out of scope (spec section 3). |
| `CommandProxy.offer`'s `Thread.yield()` and interrupt check | The producer and the consumer share the one event loop thread; see spec D42. |
| `FileStoreLogFactory`, `MappedRawLog`, `RawLog.sliceTerms` | Files and `ByteBuffer`s (spec section 3, D9, D35, D38). `MemoryLogFactory` takes their place. |
| `DriverLog.logPublicationRevoke`, `logSendChannelCreation`, `logSendChannelClose` | The logging agents are out of scope (spec section 3). |
| `DriverLog.logReceiveChannelCreation`, `logReceiveChannelClose` (`ReceiverProxy`), `DriverLog.logNakSent`-style calls in `PublicationImage` | The logging agents are out of scope (spec section 3). |
| `LossReport` creation from `loss-report.dat` in `MediaDriver.Context.conclude`, `lossReportBufferLength` use | No files (spec section 3, D50); `lossReport(report)` sets one. |
| `DriverConductor`: `ipcPublications`, `counterLinks`, `onAddIpcPublication` / `AddIpcPublicationCommand`, `onAddIpcSubscription`, `onAddSpySubscription` / `AddSpySubscriptionCommand`, `onAddRcvIpcDestination`, `onAddRcvSpyDestination` / `AddRcvSpyDestinationCommand`, `onRemoveRcvIpcOrSpyDestination`, `findSharedIpcPublication`, `getIpcPublication`, `transitionToLinger(IpcPublication)`, `cleanupIpcPublication`, `unlinkIpcSubscriptions`, `linkIpcSubscriptions`, `linkIpcSubscription`, `linkSpy`, `linkSpies`, `newIpcPublication`, `findAndUpdateResponseIpcSubscription`, `removeSubscriptionLink`, the IPC branch of `onRejectImage` and of `checkManagedResources`, `onAddCounter`, `onAddStaticCounter`, `onRemoveCounter`, `onNextAvailableSessionId` (`nextAvailableSessionId` and `advanceSessionId` are ported) | IPC, spy and counters are out of scope (spec section 3, D54, plan decision 8). The commands that name IPC or spy are an `InvalidChannelException`. |
| `MediaDriver.main`, `launchEmbedded`, `ensureDirectoryIsRecreated`, `reportExistingErrors`, `NamedCompositeAgent`, `FixedNameCompositeAgent`, the runners and thread factories of `SHARED`, `SHARED_NETWORK` and `DEDICATED`, `HighResolutionTimer` | No Aeron directory (spec D4) and no threads (D3, D54). |
| `DriverConductor.onStart`'s `DriverLog.logStart`, the `DriverLog` calls of the cleanup methods | The logging agents are out of scope (spec section 3). |
| `ExclusivePublicationLhsPadding`, `ExclusivePublicationValues`, `SubscriptionLhsPadding`, `SubscriptionFields` (the padding bytes `p000`..`p127`) | They keep the fields of two threads off one cache line; there is no field layout in JS and the client and the driver share one thread (spec D11, D19, D39). The fields are declared in `ExclusivePublication` and `Subscription`. |
| The `reverseBytes` / big-endian branches of the appends | The platform is little-endian (as `BufferClaim.commit`). |
